# Agents · I · part 11

987 entries, ingest to INSTRUCTIONS. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ingest**: Processes sources (URLs, files, text) into the knowledge base at memory/. Returns NL output for heartbeat delivery. ([spajxo/kvido](https://github.com/spajxo/kvido/blob/HEAD/agents/ingest.md) · ★2)
- **ingest**: Transform inbox files into timeline data ([bladnman/timeworm](https://github.com/bladnman/timeworm/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/agents/ingest.md))
- **ingest**: Transform research documents and enriched data files from the inbox into structured timeline JSON data that the timeworm application can visualize. ([bladnman/timeworm](https://github.com/bladnman/timeworm/blob/HEAD/open-agents/agents/ingest.md))
- **ingest**: Domain-scoped ingestion worker. Spawned by the pipeline Workflow per active domain. Reads Raw/Inbox/&lt;domain&gt;/, follows the ideas-funnel:ingest skill, proposes domain-scoped wiki pages and returns structured JSON with density signals when a concept crosses ≥3 unrelated sources. ([cosmicdreams/claude-plugins](https://github.com/cosmicdreams/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/ideas-funnel/agents/ingest.md))
- **ingest** ([Goyallgithub/Goyal_NMG_Sprint01_](https://github.com/Goyallgithub/Goyal_NMG_Sprint01_/blob/HEAD/agents/ingest.md))
- **ingest**: Classifies a RawSiteCapture into a ContentInventory, assigns section intents from the closed taxonomy, surfaces collection candidates, detects integration signals, flags ambiguities. Use as stage 2 of the Vivreal site-migration pipeline. ([HillBombCreations/vivreal-skills](https://github.com/HillBombCreations/vivreal-skills/blob/HEAD/vivreal-site-migrator/agents/ingest.md))
- **ingest**: Loads a Screaming Frog export directory and confirms the crawl size and site via the MCP load tool. ([Manish85-cell/seo-analyzer](https://github.com/Manish85-cell/seo-analyzer/blob/HEAD/seo-command-center/agents/ingest.md) · 6 copies)
- **ingest**: Expert in PDF processing, text extraction, page rendering, and chunking for the Blueprint Eye pipeline. ([R0HIT-01/BluePrint_Ai](https://github.com/R0HIT-01/BluePrint_Ai/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/ingest.md))
- **ingest**: Acquire and profile one asset. Runs in parallel per asset; never touches a shot spec. ([Ut8v/blendy](https://github.com/Ut8v/blendy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest.md) · 2 copies)
- **ingest-agent**: Fetches podcast episode transcripts from URLs and prepares them for extraction. Use PROACTIVELY when given any YouTube URL, podcast episode URL, or RSS feed link to process. ([alperkaan01/PodMind](https://github.com/alperkaan01/PodMind/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-agent.md) · ★2)
- **ingest-agent**: Stage 1 ingest via the two source adapters. Discovery, legal access resolution, parse, retraction check, manual-acquisition queue. No DB write. ([ColdMountain24/lit2db-marketplace](https://github.com/ColdMountain24/lit2db-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/lit2db/agents/ingest-agent.md))
- **ingest-agent**: Data ingestion specialist managing Firebase extraction from A3, CSV imports, API data pulls, and ETL pipeline coordination ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/tvs-microsoft-deploy/agents/ingest-agent.md))
- **ingest-agent**: Use for fetching and disk-caching raw SEC filings (Form 4, 13F-HR, DEF14A) from EDGAR. Scope is strictly network fetch + cache — no parsing, no extraction, no graph writes. ([visva-murali/FinancialKG](https://github.com/visva-murali/FinancialKG/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-agent.md))
- **ingest-api-engineer**: Background Worker (goroutine) → Ticker cada BATCH_FLUSH_MS → Si buffer &gt;= BATCH_SIZE → flush inmediato → Batch insert a ClickHouse → Retry con exponential backoff (max 3 intentos) → Log errores, nunca panic ``` ([jdrets/iSeeyou](https://github.com/jdrets/iSeeyou/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/ingest-api-engineer.md))
- **ingest-auditor**: Pass I source-only audit of the 9-pass ingestion protocol, the same-family leg. Reads one deep reference cold against its converted source, traces every claim, fixes strips, marker corrections and verbatim slips in the deep reference, and writes the Pass I audit log before stamping. Also runs report-only (an audit fixture, a second look) and quotations-only (work a `check-verbatim.py` report). Dispatched by the orchestrating session in a fresh context, never the session that wrote the deep reference; not for direct invocation. ([chrisgagne/grounded-forge](https://github.com/chrisgagne/grounded-forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-auditor.md) · ★8)
- **ingest-checker**: Audits how a PDF will be (or was) cut into figure and text chunks by the Docling pipeline, without writing to Qdrant, the registry or backend/data. Use before ingesting a new manual, after changing ingest/embed_and_store_docling.py, or when a chat answer shows the wrong picture for a figure. ([JimmyYehtut/IMKA_Knowledge_Assistant_ChatBot](https://github.com/JimmyYehtut/IMKA_Knowledge_Assistant_ChatBot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-checker.md))
- **ingest-commit**: Record a single git commit as an immutable git-commit document in an AKB vault — mechanical git metadata plus a faithful restatement of what the diff changed. ([dnotitia/akb](https://github.com/dnotitia/akb/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/akb-wiki/agents/ingest-commit.md) · ★168 · 2 copies)
- **ingest-concept-linker**: Pass H cross-link pass of the 9-pass ingestion protocol. Reads the mechanical concept-candidates payload and decides attach, merge, novel or drop for each candidate, writing a pending-decisions file (new sources into a corpus that already has decisions) or `decisions.json` (a corpus with none yet). In merge review, settles the topic linker's duplicate-concept flags instead, writing `concept-merges.json`. Dispatched by the orchestrating session; not for direct invocation. ([chrisgagne/grounded-forge](https://github.com/chrisgagne/grounded-forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-concept-linker.md) · ★8)
- **ingest-confluence**: Ingest one Confluence page into an AKB vault as a five-section LLM-wiki summary document, fetched live via the Atlassian MCP server. ([dnotitia/akb](https://github.com/dnotitia/akb/blob/HEAD/plugins/codex/akb-wiki/support/agents/ingest-confluence.md) · ★168 · 2 copies)
- **ingest-context**: Extracts atomic insights from any content Claude can read: text, PDFs, images, code, conversations, docs, or any other format. ([robinslange/learning-loop](https://github.com/robinslange/learning-loop/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/ingest-context.md) · ★13)
- **ingest-coordinator**: [DEPRECATED] Use ingest-orchestrator instead. Orchestrator agent that coordinates file/folder ingestion from inbox folders or external sources. ([datacore-one/datacore](https://github.com/datacore-one/datacore/blob/HEAD/.datacore/4-archive/agents/ingest-coordinator.md) · ★4)
- **ingest-discovery-scanner**: LLM leg of the discovery scan in the 9-pass ingestion protocol. Reads one converted source in full and adds to its mechanical-baseline `_planning/discovery/{slug}.json` the author's enumerated named methods, plus any book-index or page-marker correction the regex baseline missed. Feeds Pass H's deterministic preprocessor. Dispatched by the orchestrating session once per source after the mechanical baseline has run; not for direct invocation. ([chrisgagne/grounded-forge](https://github.com/chrisgagne/grounded-forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-discovery-scanner.md) · ★8)
- **ingest-doc**: Ingest one document (local file or web URL) into an AKB vault as a five-section LLM-wiki summary page, optionally preserving the original bytes in the raw file layer. ([dnotitia/akb](https://github.com/dnotitia/akb/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/akb-wiki/agents/ingest-doc.md) · ★168 · 2 copies)
- **ingest-engineer**: Use when building or editing DATA INGEST pipelines — ingest/ (Python dlt + DuckDB), cadence_registry, and the GHA cron wrappers that run them. Adding or normalizing a source into data_lake.*. Not for the website (website-builder), emails/PDFs (deliverable-builder), or answer behavior (answer-engine-guardian). ([ethanrickyjrjr-wq/SWFL-Data-Gulf](https://github.com/ethanrickyjrjr-wq/SWFL-Data-Gulf/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-engineer.md) · ★2)
- **ingest-implementor**: ② 입력 처리(src/task_spec/nodes/ingest/)를 구현·개선한다. PDF·스캔본·DOCX·HWPX 추출, 레이아웃 복원(2단·오른쪽 정렬 기간), 절·머리말·칸 구조화, 칸 라벨 표기 추가, OCR 설정, 새 형식 지원, 추출 평가 수치 개선 요청에서 사용. ([JangYunSeong/Wing-C-Assignment](https://github.com/JangYunSeong/Wing-C-Assignment/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-implementor.md))
- **ingest-jira**: Record one Jira issue as an atlassian-issue document in an AKB vault — title/description/resolution/comments quoted verbatim. Fetched live via the Atlassian MCP server; always upsert. ([dnotitia/akb](https://github.com/dnotitia/akb/blob/HEAD/plugins/codex/akb-wiki/support/agents/ingest-jira.md) · ★168 · 2 copies)
- **ingest-linear**: Fetches and extracts insights from Linear tickets. Pulls assigned tickets or project-scoped tickets, extracts patterns and project state. ([robinslange/learning-loop](https://github.com/robinslange/learning-loop/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/ingest-linear.md) · ★13)
- **ingest-mapper-arch**: Maps architectural layers, data flow, key abstractions, and entry points for a repository being ingested. Writes ARCH.md to _ingested-repos/&lt;slug&gt;/ with file:line citations. ([robinslange/learning-loop](https://github.com/robinslange/learning-loop/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/ingest-mapper-arch.md) · ★13)
- **ingest-mapper-conventions**: Maps coding conventions, naming patterns, import organization, testing patterns. Writes CONVENTIONS.md to _ingested-repos/&lt;slug&gt;/ with file:line citations. ([robinslange/learning-loop](https://github.com/robinslange/learning-loop/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/ingest-mapper-conventions.md) · ★13)
- **ingest-mapper-domain**: Maps the problem space, core assumptions, mental model, and anti-goals of a repository. Writes DOMAIN.md to _ingested-repos/&lt;slug&gt;/ with file:line citations. ([robinslange/learning-loop](https://github.com/robinslange/learning-loop/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/ingest-mapper-domain.md) · ★13)
- **ingest-mapper-stack**: Maps tech stack, dependencies, integrations, and runtime config for a repository being ingested. Writes STACK.md to _ingested-repos/&lt;slug&gt;/ with file:line citations. ([robinslange/learning-loop](https://github.com/robinslange/learning-loop/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/ingest-mapper-stack.md) · ★13)
- **ingest-mapper-state**: Captures ephemeral project state (current branch, recent commits, in-flight work, surface-visible concerns) from a repository being ingested. Returns inline JSON to coordinator, writes NO files. ([robinslange/learning-loop](https://github.com/robinslange/learning-loop/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/ingest-mapper-state.md) · ★13)
- **ingest-operator**: Manage batch paper ingestion with progress tracking and limited retry handling ([Minazuki02/paper-workflow](https://github.com/Minazuki02/paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-operator.md) · ★1)
- **ingest-orchestrator**: Orchestrator agent that coordinates file/folder ingestion from inbox folders or external sources. Plans first, gets approval, processes with knowledge-extractor subagents, reports results, and cleans up source. Replaces ingest-coordinator per DIP-0021. ([datacore-one/datacore](https://github.com/datacore-one/datacore/blob/HEAD/.datacore/agents/ingest-orchestrator.md) · ★4)
- **ingest-paper**: Ingests, categorizes, and summarizes a new scientific paper PDF into the project knowledge base. ([AdQuanta/AI-driven-repo---template](https://github.com/AdQuanta/AI-driven-repo---template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-paper.agent.md) · ★3)
- **ingest-pr**: Record a single GitHub PR merge event as a git-pr document in an AKB vault — PR title/body quoted verbatim, commit summaries pulled from pre-ingested git-commit docs. Fetched live via gh pr view. ([dnotitia/akb](https://github.com/dnotitia/akb/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/akb-wiki/agents/ingest-pr.md) · ★168 · 2 copies)
- **ingest-prd**: Draft docs/discovery/solution-brief.md from a customer-provided PRD, BRD, or functional spec. Produces an AI-extracted draft with per-field evidence citations and TBDs for risky fields; /discover-scenario then fills the TBDs in gap-fill mode. ([jlangley11/agentic-ai-solution-accelerator](https://github.com/jlangley11/agentic-ai-solution-accelerator/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/ingest-prd.agent.md))
- **ingest-processor**: [DEPRECATED] Use knowledge-extractor instead. Processes individual files/folders during ingestion. ([datacore-one/datacore](https://github.com/datacore-one/datacore/blob/HEAD/.datacore/4-archive/agents/ingest-processor.md) · ★4)
- **ingest-prompt-author**: Drives the per-plugin ingest skill render — owns _overrides/frontmatter.yaml, the section-targeted append markers, and the wholesale reference/ replacement files. The canonical sync skill at canonical/prompts/ingest/skills/sync/ is the source of truth; per-plugin work is overrides only. Engage when editing plugins/{slug}/skills/{slug}/_overrides/ or rendering plugins/{slug}/skills/{slug}/. ([AgntUX/AUX-plugins](https://github.com/AgntUX/AUX-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/agntux-build/agents/ingest-prompt-author.md))
- **ingest-refiler**: Pass H re-filing pass of the 9-pass ingestion protocol. Reads a batch of sources' deep references against the assembled concept index and proposes, per source, credits on existing rows, topic filings, new rows and removals of credits the source doesn't support, writing `refile/refile-{batch}.json` for `refile_sources` to apply. Run after a large ingest or when routing misses sources that cover a question. Dispatched by the orchestrating session; not for direct invocation. ([chrisgagne/grounded-forge](https://github.com/chrisgagne/grounded-forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-refiler.md) · ★8)
- **ingest-refs-extractor**: Pass H refs pass of the 9-pass ingestion protocol. Reads one deep reference's header and thesis and writes its reference-index fields (author, year, title, primary_topic, concept_tags) to `_planning/staging/{corpus}/refs/{slug}.json`. Dispatched by the orchestrating session once per source after the deep reference is written; not for direct invocation. ([chrisgagne/grounded-forge](https://github.com/chrisgagne/grounded-forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-refs-extractor.md) · ★8)
- **ingest-release**: Record a single release tag as a git-release document in an AKB vault — release notes from the matching GitHub Release (annotated tag message fallback), commits bucketed by conv_type. Requires range commits pre-ingested. ([dnotitia/akb](https://github.com/dnotitia/akb/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/akb-wiki/agents/ingest-release.md) · ★168 · 2 copies)
- **ingest-repo**: Scans a repository and extracts architecture, stack, patterns, and project context as insights. ([robinslange/learning-loop](https://github.com/robinslange/learning-loop/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/ingest-repo.md) · ★13)
- **ingest-reviewer**: 収録（ingest）作業のレビュー専用エージェント。別モデル（Opus 等）で進めた収録の成果物一式（registry・parsed・normalized・gen・アプリ配線・docs 追記）を、検証ゲートが守らない領域を中心に検査して所見を報告する。ファイルは一切書き換えない。収録 PR を作る直前・パーサ本体を改修した後・新様式の初収録時に1体起動する。frontmatter でモデルを Opus（Opus 5.5）に固定しているため、セッションをどのモデルで回していてもこのエージェントは Opus 5.5 以上の水準で走る。 ([philosophy-house/budget-trace](https://github.com/philosophy-house/budget-trace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-reviewer.md))
- **ingest-reviewer**: Independent read-only reviewer for the transcription of one acquired document in a Yllir campaign. Re-reads the original and compares it line by line with the transcription, and returns the outcome of each line as a JSON array. ([StefanoVe/yllir](https://github.com/StefanoVe/yllir/blob/HEAD/yllir/agents/ingest-reviewer.md))
- **ingest-source**: Generic source ingestion for the research wiki. Handles web articles, PDFs, papers, GitHub readmes, pasted text, and other non-YouTube sources. Detects source type, fetches content, and hands off to the karpathy-llm-wiki ingest workflow under the editorial schema. Use when the user wants to add a non-YouTube source to their wiki. ([rearc/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/rearc/ai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/packages/rearc-research-kit/.apm/agents/ingest-source.md) · ★2)
- **ingest-specialist**: Data pipeline specialist for stupid-db. Deep expertise in the ingest, connector, and embedder crates. Handles Parquet/Arrow ingestion, entity extraction, edge derivation, embedding generation, field normalization, file watching, and remote data reading. Use for pipeline work. ([FrancisVarga/stupid-db](https://github.com/FrancisVarga/stupid-db/blob/HEAD/packages/stupid-claude-agent/.claude/agents/ingest-specialist.md))
- **ingest-supervisor**: Coordinates the initial ingest phase of the bug workflow including Scout, Dossier, and Branch creation. ([Konstantilieris/Bugfow_Plugin](https://github.com/Konstantilieris/Bugfow_Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/ingest-supervisor.md) · ★1)
- **ingest-synthesizer**: Merges 4 structured mapper docs + state sidecar JSON into confirmed_insights JSON for the existing route-output pipeline. Read-only; no file writes. ([robinslange/learning-loop](https://github.com/robinslange/learning-loop/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/ingest-synthesizer.md) · ★13)
- **ingest-test**: Test Agent Content ([CODESLAYER-X86/x86-kit](https://github.com/CODESLAYER-X86/x86-kit/blob/HEAD/.x86-kit/agents/ingest-test.md))
- **ingest-topic-linker**: Pass H topic pass of the 9-pass ingestion protocol. Reads the assembled concept vocabulary and files every concept under topics (subject topics and cross-school debates), keeps only true synonyms, and flags duplicate concepts for merging, writing `_planning/staging/{corpus}/concepts/topics.json`. For corpora too large for one agent, runs one stage of the staged pass (propose, file one chunk, or consolidate). Dispatched by the orchestrating session after the concept index is assembled; not for direct invocation. ([chrisgagne/grounded-forge](https://github.com/chrisgagne/grounded-forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-topic-linker.md) · ★8)
- **ingest-worker**: Ingests a single raw/ source file into the wiki knowledge base. Use when parallelizing bulk ingestion — the orchestrator detects unprocessed files and dispatches one worker per file. ([Berkay2002/wikillm](https://github.com/Berkay2002/wikillm/blob/HEAD/agents/ingest-worker.md))
- **ingest-worker**: ../../../agents/llm-wiki-mgmt/ingest-worker.md ([ISaidHey/isaidhey-agent-skills](https://github.com/ISaidHey/isaidhey-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/llm-wiki-mgmt/agents/ingest-worker.md))
- **ingest-worker**: Processes ONE queued session digest from +/_sessions/&lt;layer&gt;/&lt;project&gt;.md into its &lt;layer&gt;/projects/ note, in an isolated context. Spawn one per digest when fanning out /aios-ingest; the orchestrator handles Knowledge Map, Log, the leak gate, archiving, and the commit. Prefer the fast model tier — this is mechanical synthesis. ([sublimecoder/aios](https://github.com/sublimecoder/aios/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest-worker.md))
- **ingest-youtube**: Ingest a YouTube video into the research wiki. Fetches the title and transcript, hands the content to the karpathy-llm-wiki ingest workflow, and ensures the editorial schema applies. Use when the user wants to add a YouTube video to their wiki. ([rearc/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/rearc/ai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/packages/rearc-research-kit/.apm/agents/ingest-youtube.md) · ★2)
- **ingest.agent**: These agents are invoked by `/ingest-full` and `/ingest-incremental` commands. Each agent has a single responsibility. Commands orchestrate them in sequence. ([abd-abdur/sdd-harness](https://github.com/abd-abdur/sdd-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingest.agent.md))
- **ingesta-validacion**: Lee y valida documentos externos (extractos bancarios, facturas XML/PDF, archivos de cotizaciones CSV/JSON) antes de que toquen la base de datos. Úsalo cuando llegue un archivo nuevo o datos de una fuente externa. ([saorionline/optim-ryo-ia](https://github.com/saorionline/optim-ryo-ia/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingesta-validacion.md))
- **ingestao-bancaria**: Implementa e corrige a entrada de dados: cliente Pluggy, sync, parsers OFX/CSV, CLI `gastos`, routers FastAPI e cliente OpenAI. Use para qualquer tarefa em api/src/gastos/ingest/, api/src/gastos/api/, api/src/gastos/insights/openai_client.py ou cli.py. ([Doglas-Anjos/controle-de-gastos](https://github.com/Doglas-Anjos/controle-de-gastos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingestao-bancaria.md))
- **ingester**: Use this agent to ingest a source file from raw/ into the wiki. Reads the file, identifies entities, creates or updates wiki pages following the strict format, surfaces contradictions, updates index.md and log.md. Never modifies raw/. Never writes to output/. ([evb87-tech/corpus](https://github.com/evb87-tech/corpus/blob/HEAD/corpus-core/agents/ingester.md) · ★1)
- **ingester**: Lee una fuente larga (documentación extensa, un paper, un repo de experimento con muchos resultados) y devuelve solo la lista de hechos concretos con su ubicación. Lo usan /ingest, /harvest y /capture para no llenar el contexto principal. No escribe en el repo. ([falkenslab/falkens-notebook](https://github.com/falkenslab/falkens-notebook/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingester.md) · ★1)
- **ingester**: Takes one document apart into amemo records with sources. Use it for long files (standards, catalogues, reports, spreadsheets), so the document text does not fill the main conversation. ([empty-complete/amemo](https://github.com/empty-complete/amemo/blob/HEAD/amemo/agents/plugin/agents/ingester.md))
- **ingester**: Processes dropped documents: reading, filing, linking, comment, deadlines triggered. ([nedjo90/juriste-fiduciaire-mustafa-ekrem](https://github.com/nedjo90/juriste-fiduciaire-mustafa-ekrem/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingester.md))
- **ingester**: Reads one chunk of a study-kit course's lecture material (at most 20 pages of one PDF) - the extracted page text first, the PDF page as an image only for flagged pages, scanned pages transcribed - and writes its digest sources/notes/&lt;chunk&gt;.md following the ingest skill. Returns compact JSON. Used by /register-new-subject and /add-material, one chunk per call. ([SemperParatusGithub/study-kit](https://github.com/SemperParatusGithub/study-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ingester.md))
- **ingester**: Reads raw log files, detects format, normalises entries, and writes output/rca_raw.json ([suryav0104/enterprise-ai-control-plane](https://github.com/suryav0104/enterprise-ai-control-plane/blob/HEAD/agents/ingester.md))
- **ingester-conversation**: You extract concepts from a conversation with a domain expert into concepts.yaml format. ([YeechingTiger/ontology-builder-agent](https://github.com/YeechingTiger/ontology-builder-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/ontology-agent/agents/ingester-conversation.md) · ★3)
- **ingester-datadict**: You extract concepts from a data dictionary into the standard concepts.yaml format. ([YeechingTiger/ontology-builder-agent](https://github.com/YeechingTiger/ontology-builder-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/ontology-agent/agents/ingester-datadict.md) · ★3)
- **ingester-document**: You extract concepts from unstructured text (papers, protocols, specs) into concepts.yaml format. ([YeechingTiger/ontology-builder-agent](https://github.com/YeechingTiger/ontology-builder-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/ontology-agent/agents/ingester-document.md) · ★3)
- **ingester-owl**: You extract concepts from an existing OWL/RDF ontology file into concepts.yaml format. ([YeechingTiger/ontology-builder-agent](https://github.com/YeechingTiger/ontology-builder-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/ontology-agent/agents/ingester-owl.md) · ★3)
- **ingestion**: Import server integration from `@happyvertical/smrt-education/ingestion`, never from browser code. Build IngestionService, SubmissionService and the supplied scope from one authenticated server session. Derive learner/enrollment selection from authorized application state, not OCR output or uploaded metadata. ([happyvertical/smrt](https://github.com/happyvertical/smrt/blob/HEAD/packages/education/agents/ingestion.md) · ★7)
- **ingestion**: Use proactively for any ingestion into this vault — YouTube videos, transcripts, web clippings, PDFs — whether triggered manually or by automation. MUST BE USED as the entry point instead of hand-rolling a one-off ingestion flow, so every source type lands through the same pipeline into `60_Claude/05_Clippings` / `60_Claude/10_Source_Summaries` and gets picked up by `research-distiller` consistently. - **inspire-applier**: Applies the edits /apply has already gotten the user to approve. Given a list of approved doc edits, it writes each one via the bundled inspire-docs write_doc tool — which is path-bounded to the project's docs directory — and reports what landed. Write-restricted by design: its only write path is write_doc, so it physically cannot edit files outside the docs directory or touch the inspiration corpus. ([skqr/claude-inspire-plugin](https://github.com/skqr/claude-inspire-plugin/blob/HEAD/inspire/agents/inspire-applier.md))
- **inspire-reader**: Reads a single non-YouTube web page for the /inspire skill — pulls its main text via the bundled inspire-content MCP and evaluates the page against the project it's running in (what it's about, what we can learn, what's directly applicable). Read-only and minimal-egress by design: it reads untrusted third-party page text, so it has no shell, cannot write or edit files, and its only network reach is the SSRF-guarded webpage tool. ([skqr/claude-inspire-plugin](https://github.com/skqr/claude-inspire-plugin/blob/HEAD/inspire/agents/inspire-reader.md))
- **inspire-scribe**: Writes the corpus files /inspire has already composed. Given the exact path and full contents of each inspiration note (and the corpus README), it writes them via the bundled inspire-docs write_note tool — which is path-bounded to the inspiration corpus (&lt;docs&gt;/inspiration/) — and reports what landed. Write-restricted by design: its only write path is write_note, so it physically cannot touch the project's own docs or anything else outside the corpus. ([skqr/claude-inspire-plugin](https://github.com/skqr/claude-inspire-plugin/blob/HEAD/inspire/agents/inspire-scribe.md))
- **inspire-watcher**: Watches a single YouTube video for the /inspire skill — pulls its transcript via the bundled inspire-content MCP and evaluates the video against the project it's running in (what it's about, what we can learn, what's directly applicable). - **instruction-audit-2026-10-04**: 本次在 [9 月 7 日审计](instruction-audit-2026-09-07.md) 的基础上处理触发冲突、重复澄清和无关材料加载，不修改业务实现。依据为 [OpenAI GPT-6 指南](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/latest-model?model=gpt-6-astra#gpt-6-astra-testing-and-verification) 与 [Rethinking skills and prompts for GPT-6 Astra](https://x.com/pvncher/status/2095991462416490862)。 ([Tenon-Net/TenonAdmin](https://github.com/Tenon-Net/TenonAdmin/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/instruction-audit-2026-10-04.md) · ★45)
- **instruction-auditor**: Read-only audit of a repository's agent instruction files (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, rules, Cursor and Copilot instructions, GEMINI.md, skill descriptions). - **instruction-structure-is-role-rules-steps**: The runtime treats instructions as the agent's standing prompt. A one-line goal produces inconsistent navigation. That structure is BC-specific, not generic prompt flavour.
